Maybe use a "flow" algorithm: At each step, identify max m. It involves pairs (i,m-i). We can reduce c_m by moving mass from one element of a contributing pair to an element not involved in m. This is like reducing the product. Since nonnegative, for pair (i,j=i? or i,j with i+j=m), product a_i a_j. Moving mass from a_i to a_k not in slice reduces c_m by a_j δ (linear) but may increase other c's by a_k times neighbor. We can choose k to minimize local increases. This is a combinatorial local search.

We can implement a simple greedy reduction:
- For current max m, compute contributions x_i = a_i * a_{m-i}. Sum M.
- Find a pair (i,m-i) with high contribution and a safe position k not in slice such that adding δ there doesn't create large new contributions. Move δ from i to k.
- This is essentially a feasible descent for the max constraint.

    n=a.size; F=next_pow2(max(2*n,3*n)) maybe 3n.
    best = a.copy(); best_score = objective_fft(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # momentum
    v = zeros(n)
    beta_m=0.9
    power=2.0
    # schedule: for time, increase power slowly from 1 to maybe 200
    it=0
    while time < deadline:
       # maybe every 200 iter increase power: power = min(power_max, power*1.05) or linear
       c = conv_fft(a,F)
       cmax = c.max()
       # If cmax <= target? still optimize
       # weights
       ratio = c / cmax
       # power may be float; p = ratio**power
       p = np.power(ratio, power)
       p += 1e-30
       p /= p.sum()
       # G via crosscorr
       Arev = a[::-1]
       Pfft = rfft(p, F2)
       Arfft = rfft(Arev, F2)
       cross = irfft(Pfft*Arfft, F2)[:3*n-1]
       G = 2.0 * cross[n-1 : 2*n-1]
       Gdot = np.dot(G,a)
       h = a * (G - Gdot)
       # normalize h
       hsum = np.sum(np.abs(h))
       if hsum < 1e-15: break or perturb
       v = beta_m * v + h
       vsum = np.sum(np.abs(v))
       step = step0 / (1.0 + it/1000) maybe

Algorithm for one optimizer run:
```
a normalized sum=1
v = zeros
best=score_fft
it=0
K=4; power_k=1
while time:
  c = conv_full(a)
  cmax=c.max()
  # take top K indices, K schedule maybe min(128, max(2,int(2+(it/decay))))
  top = argpartition(c, K-1)[:K] random shuffle? 
  # weights: maybe powers of ratio within top
  r = c[top]/cmax
  w = r ** power_k  (or softmax)
  w /= sum
  G = zeros(n)
  for idx,wgt in zip(top,w):
      m=int(idx)
      k_min=max(0,m-n+1); k_max=min(m,n-1)
      if k_max>=k_min:
         G[k_min:k_max+1] += wgt * 2.0 * a[m-k_max : m-k_min+1][::-1]
  Gdot = dot(G,a)
  h = a*(G-Gdot)
  hnorm=sum(abs(h))
  if hnorm<eps:
     # random perturbation or break
  v = beta*v + h
  vnorm=sum(abs(v))
  delta = step * v / (vnorm+eps)
  a *= exp(-clip(delta,-5,5))
  clip a min 1e-15? normalize
  # if score improves update
```

Let's implement `objective_and_gradient_full(a, F, power)`:
- A = rfft(a,F); C=irfft(A*A,F)[:L]; C = maximum(C,0)
- cmax=C.max(); score=2*n*cmax (sum=1)
- ratio=C/cmax (if cmax>0 else zeros)
- w = ratio**power; but for power maybe float up to 500. If many underflow to 0, okay. To keep smooth, add `w = w + 1e-12`; w /= w.sum()`
- G: cross-correlation. Define `Arev = a[::-1]`; `P = rfft(w, F)`; `AR = rfft(Arev, F)`; `cross = irfft(P*AR, F)`; `G = 2.0 * cross[n-1:2*n-1]` (length n: indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive = 2n-1 - (n-1)? Wait earlier cross length needs take slice `n-1 : 2*n-1` because Python stop exclusive. For n=10, n-1=9 to 19 exclusive length10 (9..18). 2n-1=19 yes length n. Good.
- Gdot = dot(G,a)
- h = a*(G - Gdot)
Return score,cmax,h.

Check cross index for k=n-1: t= n-1+k = 2n-2. Stop 2n-1 exclusive includes 2n-2. Good.

Need F large enough for cross: len(cross)=3n-2; F>=3n-2. For n=4498, 3n-2=13492 ->16384. Good. If n up to maybe 10000, F=32768 still okay.

Memory: arrays length F ~16384, fine.

```
def optimize_shape(a0, time_budget, start_iteration=0, power_start=2, power_max=200, rng=None):
    n=a0.size; F=next_pow2(max(2*n-1, 3*n-2))
    a = normalize(a0)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=objective_fft(a,F)
    v=zeros; it=0; no_imp=0; power=power_start; beta=0.9
    # Maybe record local best.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # schedule power
       power = min(power_max, power_start + (it)/500.0) if it<... else power_max
       score, cmax, h = compute_gradient(a,F,power)
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_imp=0
       else: no_imp +=1
       hv = v + h? Use v = beta*v + (1-beta)*h? But h normalized? We'll normalize h first.
       hnorm=sum(abs(h)); if hnorm<1e-18: perturb/break
       h = h/hnorm? If we normalize h here, then v direction; okay. But if normalize h before momentum, scale doesn't matter. We can normalize h to L1=1 then v.
       v = beta*v + h
       vnorm=sum(abs(v)); if vnorm<1e-18: perturb
       step = max(0.05, 0.3/(1+it/2000))
       delta = step * v / vnorm
       delta = np.clip(delta, -8,8)
       a = a * exp(-delta)
       # protect zeros: if any a==0? set min? Use `np.maximum(a, 1e-300)`. But after many steps, some may underflow. We can add small floor relative to sum: `a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12 / n?)`? Setting floor may ruin sparsity, but n=4500, 1e-12 each sum4e-9 negligible. Use min 1e-15.
       a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
       s=a.sum(); if s<=0: break
       a /= s
       it +=1
       if no_imp > 4000:
           # perturb
           noise_level = 0.5 + 0.5*rng.random()
           a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise_level, n))
           # optional roll
           if rng.random()<0.5: a = np.roll(a, int(rng.integers(1,n)))
           a = np.maximum(a,1e-15); a/=a.sum()
           v.fill(0); power = max(power_start, power/4); no_imp=0; step maybe reset
    return best,best_score
```

```
def fast_score(a, F):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return inf
    a=a/s
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=F)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=F)[:2*len(a)-1]
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    return 2*len(a)*cmax
```
For n=4498,F=16384, this is okay.

But in optimizer, we compute c and h; no need fast_score.

Random Fourier:
```
def make_fourier(n, rng, nterms=8, beta=0.6):
    j=np.arange(n)
    s=zeros
    for r in range(nreg):
       f=int(rng.integers(1,n))
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       amp=np.exp(rng.normal(-0.5,0.5))
       s += amp*np.cos(2π*f*j/n + phase)
    s -= s.mean()
    # scale to max ~1
    m=np.max(np.abs(s))
    if m>0: s=s/m
    a=1 + beta*s
    a=np.maximum(a, 0.05)
    return a/a.sum()
```
This can produce positive sequence.

But type introspection without isinstance? allowed. No lambdas. This might be viewed okay. However some frameworks inspect function signature and expect specific? Usually they just call `propose_candidate()`. Flexible is safer. Let's implement:
```
def propose_candidate(*args, **kwargs):
    seed = 42
    budget_s = 1800.0
    init_seq = None
    if len(args) > 0 and isinstance(args[0], (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
        init_seq = args[0]
    elif len(args) > 0 and isinstance(args[0], (int, float)) and not isinstance(args[0], bool):
        seed = int(args[0]) if isinstance(args[0], int) else 42
    if len(args) > 1:
        val = args[1]
        if isinstance(val, (int,float)) and not isinstance(val,bool):
            budget_s = float(val)
        elif isinstance(val, (list,tuple,np.ndarray)):
            init_seq = val
    # kwargs: height_sequence_1, seed, budget_s
```

Let's estimate if two-exponential can get target. We can reason continuous and maybe optimum. Suppose f(x)=e^{-αx}+γ e^{-α(1-x)} normalized. It has high left, lower right. For γ small, left-left peak at y≈1/α (if α>1). Adding γ right tail adds cross terms at left y: ∫_0^y e^{-αx} γ e^{-α(1-x)} dx = γ e^{-α} ∫_0^y 1 dx = γ e^{-α} y, small; and right-right at small y: γ² e^{-2α(1-x)} ~γ² e^{-2α} y, tiny. So left peak not reduced; it's caused by left tail. To reduce left peak, must lower f near 0 relative to its mass, i.e., make f(x) have a hole near 0? But then mass shifts right; convolution peak moves. Exponential peak at boundary? For α=1.3, peak y=0.77, which is not boundary; f(x) at x=0.77 has e^{-1}=0.367, not too high. The convolution peak is product of mid-values. To reduce, make f more U-shaped? If f low in middle, convolution at y=0.77 (sum of x around0.77?) Wait y=0.77: pairs x and 0.77-x, both in [0,0.77]. For f decreasing, high at x=0 values but pair x=0 uses f(0.77) low, x=0.38 uses f(0.39) mid. The integral balances. A shape with a valley might lower this. Could be optimized.

A roll of exponential (wrap) creates a sequence with a jump discontinuity: high at one end, low, then high wrap? Actually rolling a decaying exponential creates high block near one boundary and a discontinuity; could be beneficial because it places mass at both ends? Let's detail `_compute_log_gradient`:
```
def _compute_log_gradient(a, F, weight_power):
    n = a.size
    L = 2*n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=F)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=F)[:L]
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
    cmax = float(np.max(C)) if cmax? 
    score = 2.0 * n * cmax
    if cmax <= 0: return score, zeros
    ratio = C / cmax
    # weight_power may be float >=1
    W = ratio ** weight_power
    # If all W zero due underflow except cmax? ratio max=1 so W max=1
    wsum = W.sum()
    if wsum <= 0 or not finite:
        W = np.zeros(L); W[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0
    else:
        W /= wsum
    # cross corr
    Arev = a[::-1]
    WA = np.fft.rfft(W, n=F)
    AR = np.fft.rfft(Arev, n=F)
    cross = np.fft.irfft(WA * AR, n=F)
    # G slice
    G = 2.0 * cross[n-1 : 2*n-1]
    # Due FFT, G might have tiny negative? no matter
    Gdot = float(np.dot(G, a))
    H = a * (G - Gdot)
    return score, H
```
Note: If weight_power=1 and W = C/cmax normalized, W sum=1. For sum of C constant, W maybe not derivative of a simple objective but okay.

Potential issue: W length L, padded to F; cross-correlation formula assumes P length L and zero padding. Good.

Direct score `score` from c before gradient. The gradient corresponds to weights from this c. Good.

Step normalization: `h` may contain NaNs if G/FFT overflow? Values bounded. Use checks.

```
def _optimize_shape(a0, F, deadline, rng):
    a = _clip_normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    best = a.copy()
    score, h = _compute_log_gradient(a, F, 1.0)
    best_score = score
    v = np.zeros(n)
    beta = 0.9
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    power = 1.0  # weight exponent
    # Maybe force initial power 2? Use power=1.0 or 2.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # schedule power: increase
       power = min(80.0, 1.0 + it/1000.0) *? Actually it/1000 gives 1 at start, 2 at1000, 81 at80000.
       # Use power = 1 + 10*(1-exp(-it/3000))? Simpler linear.
       score, h = _compute_log_gradient(a,F,power)
       if finite and score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best = a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
       else: no_improve += 1
       hn = np.sum(np.abs(h))
       if not finite or hn < 1e-18:
           perturbation
       else:
           h = h / hn
           v = beta * v + h
           vn = np.sum(np.abs(v))
           if vn < 1e-18: v=h; vn=hn
           step = max(0.05, 0.30 / (1.0 + it/3000.0))
           delta = step * v / vn
           # clip
           np.clip(delta, -8.0, 8.0, out=delta)
           a *= np.exp(-delta)
           # ensure positive and finite
           a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 1e-15)
           s = a.sum()
           if s <= 0 or not finite: a=best.copy(); v.fill(0); continue
           a /= s
       it += 1
       if no_improve > 5000:
           # perturbation
           noise = 0.4 + 0.6*rng.random()
           a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise, n))
           if rng.random() < 0.3:
              shift=int(rng.integers(1,n))
              a=np.roll(a, shift)
           if rng.random() < 0.3:
              a=a[::-1]
           a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); a/=a.sum()
           v.fill(0)
           no_improve=0
           # reset step? power = min(power, 5)
           power = min(power, 5.0)
    return best, best_score
```

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    x = 1
    while x < m:
        x <<= 1
    return x

`_clip_normalize`:
```
def _clip_normalize(arr):
    try:
       a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if a.ndim != 1:
       a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
       return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return None
    a = a / s
    return a
```

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(a, F):
    a = _clip_normalize(a)
    if a is None: return np.inf
    n=a.size
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, n=F)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=F)[:2*n-1]
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0: 
       cmax=max(0,cmax)
    return float(2*n*cmax)
```
No need normalize again if passed normalized, but safe. This clones.

`_make_two_exp`:
```
def _make_two_exp(n, alpha, beta, gamma, reverse=False):
    if n <=0: return None
    if n ==1: return np.array([1.0])
    if reverse:
        t = 1.0 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)/(n-1.0)
    else:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)/(n-1.0)
    a = np.exp(-alpha * t)
    if gamma > 0:
       a = a + gamma * np.exp(-beta * (1.0 - t))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return a/s
```
Note if n=2, t [0,1].

def _make_power_curve(n, alpha, p, reverse=False):
   t...
   if reverse t=1-t
   a=np.exp(-alpha * np.power(t, p))
```

`_make_fourier`:
```
def _make_fourier(n, rng, n_terms=8, strength=0.6):
    j=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    s=np.zeros(n)
    for r in range(n_terms):
       f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n)))
       phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2*math.pi))
       amp=float(np.exp(rng.normal(-0.5,0.5)))
       s += amp * np.cos((2.0*math.pi*f*j)/max(1,n) + phase)
    s -= s.mean()
    m=float(np.max(np.abs(s)))
    if m > 0: s = s/m
    a = 1.0 + strength*s
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.05)
    a = a / a.sum()
    return a
```
Need import math.

def _append_candidate(a, F, scored, max_count):
    if len(scored) >= max_count: return False
    ar = _clip_normalize(a)
    if ar is None: return False
    sc = _fast_score(ar, F)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
       scored.append((sc, ar))
       return True
    return False
```

Let's refine `_optimize_shape` with time guard and precomputes:
```
def _optimize_shape(a0, F, deadline, rng):
    a = _clip_normalize(a0)
    if a is None: return None, np.inf
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    best=a.copy(); best_score, h = _compute_log_gradient(a,F,1.0)
    if not finite best_score: best_score = _fast_score(a,F)
    v=np.zeros(n); beta=0.9; local_it=0; no_improve=0; restarts=0
    last_print=0.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        weight_power = min(150.0, 1.0 + 0.5 + local_it/500.0)  # start 1.5? maybe 1.0
        # maybe start 1.5 for smooth
        score, h = _compute_log_gradient(a,F,weight_power)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
            else:
                no_improve +=1
        else:
            no_improve +=1
        hn = float(np.sum(np.abs(h)))
        if (not np.isfinite(hn)) or hn < 1e-18:
            # perturb
        else:
            h = h / hn
            v = beta * v + h
            vn = float(np.sum(np.abs(v)))
            if vn < 1e-18:
                v = h; vn=hn
            step = max(0.04, 0.25 / (1.0 + local_it/2000.0))
            delta = step * v / vn
            np.clip(delta, -8.0, 8.0, out=delta)
            a *= np.exp(-delta)
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 1e-15)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                a = best.copy(); v.fill(0)
            else:
                a /= s
        local_it +=1
        # print every 20s
        now=time.monotonic(); if now-last_print >20: print...
        if no_improve > max(2000, local_it//2):
            restarts +=1
            noise = min(1.8, 0.35 + 0.18*restarts)
            a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                shift = int(rng.integers(1, n))
                a = np.roll(a, shift)
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                a = a[::-1]
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
            s=a.sum()
            if s>0: a/=s
            v.fill(0)
            local_it=0
            no_improve=0
    return best, best_score
```

def propose_candidate(*args, **kwargs):
    seed=42; budget_s=1800.0; init_seq=None
    if len(args)>0:
       val=args[0]
       if isinstance(val, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
           init_seq=val

       if init_seq is None: init_seq=val; seq_found=True
   elif isinstance(val,(int,float)) and not isinstance(val,bool):
       numeric_vals.append(val)
if len(numeric_vals)>=2:
   seed=int(numeric_vals[0]) if isinstance(numeric_vals[0], int) else 42
   budget_s=float(numeric_vals[-1])
elif len(numeric_vals)==1:
   if seq_found:
       budget_s=float(numeric_vals[0])
   else:
       seed=int(numeric_vals[0]) if isinstance(numeric_vals[0], int) else 42
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
import gc


"""
Minimize J = 2n * max(f*f) / (sum f)^2 for a nonnegative length-n sequence.

Algorithm:
- Work on the simplex, sum(a)=1, so J = 2n * max(autocorrelation).
- Start from many cheap parametric candidates (exponential ramps, mixed ramps,
  power and rational decays, smooth random Fourier profiles) and select the best
  ones by FFT-based scoring.
- Polish each top candidate with a log-space projected/subgradient optimizer.
  For a smooth power-mean surrogate of the peak autocorrelation, use the exact
  gradient in log coordinates:
      h_k = a_k * (G_k - sum_i a_i G_i),
  where G is the gradient of the surrogate with respect to a.
  Updates are multiplicative:
      a <- a * exp(-eta * momentum(h)), followed by renormalization.
- If optimization stalls, perturb with multiplicative noise, rolling, and reversal.
- Directly verify the best final candidates with evaluate_sequence and return
  the best verified sequence (or the best estimated sequence if no direct
  verification was possible before the time limit).
"""


def _tuple_score(t):
    return t[0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    x = 1
    while x < m:
        x <<= 1
    return x


def _clip_normalize(arr):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    a = a / s
    return a


def _fast_score(a, F):
    a = _clip_normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=F)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=F)
    if c.size >= 2 * n - 1:
        c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf
    if cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax)


def _make_two_exp(n, alpha, beta, gamma, reverse=False):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if reverse:
        t = 1.0 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * t)
    if gamma > 0.0:
        a = a + float(gamma) * np.exp(-float(beta) * (1.0 - t))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _make_power_curve(n, alpha, p, reverse=False):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if reverse:
        t = 1.0 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * np.power(t, float(p)))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _make_poly_inverse(n, alpha, q, reverse=False):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if reverse:
        t = 1.0 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    a = np.power(1.0 + float(alpha) * t, -float(q))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _make_fourier(n, rng, n_terms=8, strength=0.65):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = max(1, n)
    for _ in range(n_terms):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n)))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(np.exp(rng.normal(-0.5, 0.5)))
        s += amp * np.cos((2.0 * math.pi * f * j) / float(denom) + phase)
    s -= s.mean()
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(s)))
    if m > 0.0 and np.isfinite(m):
        s = s / m
    a = 1.0 + float(strength) * s
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.05)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.05)
    o = float(a.sum())
    if o <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(o):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / o


def _make_block_profile(n, rng):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    choices = [4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256]
    block = int(choices[int(rng.integers(0, len(choices)))])
    nb = (n + block - 1) // block
    amps = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, nb))
    a = np.repeat(amps, block)[:n]
    a += 0.25 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _append_candidate(a, F, scored, max_count):
    if len(scored) >= max_count:
        return
    ar = _clip_normalize(a)
    if ar is None:
        return
    sc = _fast_score(ar, F)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        scored.append((sc, ar))


def _generate_candidates(n, F, seed, gen_deadline, init_arr):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    scored = []
    max_cand = 520

    if init_arr is not None:
        _append_candidate(init_arr, F, scored, max_cand)
    _append_candidate(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), F, scored, max_cand)

    # Exponential / mixed exponential ramps.
    a_start = 0.9
    a_stop = 1.801
    a_step = 0.05
    alpha = a_start
    while alpha <= a_stop:
        gamma = 0.0
        gamma_list = [0.0, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0]
        for g in gamma_list:
            if time.monotonic() >= gen_deadline:
                break
            _append_candidate(_make_two_exp(n, alpha, alpha, g, False), F, scored, max_cand)
            if len(scored) < max_cand and g != 1.0:
                _append_candidate(_make_two_exp(n, alpha, alpha, g, True), F, scored, max_cand)
        alpha += a_step

    # Power-law exponential decays.
    for alpha in [0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.6, 2.0]:
        for p in [0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0]:
            if time.monotonic() >= gen_deadline:
                break
            _append_candidate(_make_power_curve(n, alpha, p, False), F, scored, max_cand)
            _append_candidate(_make_power_curve(n, alpha, p, True), F, scored, max_cand)

    # Rational inverse decays.
    for alpha in [1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0]:
        for q in [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]:
            if time.monotonic() >= gen_deadline:
                break
            _append_candidate(_make_poly_inverse(n, alpha, q, False), F, scored, max_cand)
            _append_candidate(_make_poly_inverse(n, alpha, q, True), F, scored, max_cand)

    # Smooth random Fourier profiles.
    for _ in range(70):
        if time.monotonic() >= gen_deadline or len(scored) >= max_cand:
            break
        n_terms = int(rng.integers(4, 13))
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.95))
        _append_candidate(_make_fourier(n, rng, n_terms=n_terms, strength=strength), F, scored, max_cand)

    # Block profiles.
    for _ in range(30):
        if time.monotonic() >= gen_deadline or len(scored) >= max_cand:
            break
        _append_candidate(_make_block_profile(n, rng), F, scored, max_cand)

    scored.sort(key=_tuple_score)
    return scored


def _compute_log_gradient(a, F, weight_power):
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=F)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=F)[:L]
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
    cmax = float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return score, h

    ratio = C / cmax
    # weight_power corresponds to exponent (p-1) in the derivative of an L^p surrogate.
    W = np.power(ratio, float(weight_power))
    wsum = float(np.sum(W))
    if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
        W = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        W[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
    else:
        W /= wsum

    Arev = a[::-1]
    WA = np.fft.rfft(W, n=F)
    AR = np.fft.rfft(Arev, n=F)
    cross = np.fft.irfft(WA * AR, n=F)
    G = 2.0 * cross[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    Gdot = float(np.dot(G, a))
    h = a * (G - Gdot)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return score, h


def _optimize_shape(a0, F, deadline, rng):
    a = _clip_normalize(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf

    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_score, h = _compute_log_gradient(a, F, 1.0)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = _fast_score(a, F)
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            return a, np.inf

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = 0.90
    local_it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    restarts = 0
    last_print = 0.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        weight_power = min(150.0, 1.0 + float(local_it) / 500.0)

        score, h = _compute_log_gradient(a, F, weight_power)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = score
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        hn = float(np.sum(np.abs(h)))
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now - last_print > 25.0:
            print(f"optimize iter={local_it} score={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} wp={weight_power:.2f}", flush=True)
            last_print = now

        if (not np.isfinite(hn)) or hn < 1e-18:
            noise = min(1.8, 0.45)
            a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                a /= s
            v.fill(0)
            no_improve = 0
            local_it = 0
        else:
            h = h / hn
            v = beta * v + h
            vn = float(np.sum(np.abs(v)))
            if not np.isfinite(vn) or vn < 1e-18:
                v = h
                vn = hn
            step = max(0.04, 0.25 / (1.0 + float(local_it) / 2000.0))
            delta = step * v / vn
            np.clip(delta, -8.0, 8.0, out=delta)
            a = a * np.exp(-delta)
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 1e-15)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                a = best.copy()
                v.fill(0)
            else:
                a = a / s

        local_it += 1

        if no_improve > max(2000, local_it // 2):
            restarts += 1
            noise = min(1.8, 0.35 + 0.18 * restarts)
            a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                shift = int(rng.integers(1, n))
                a = np.roll(a, shift)
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                a = a[::-1]
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                a /= s
            v.fill(0)
            local_it = 0
            no_improve = 0

    return best, best_score


def _safe_evaluate(arr):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        val = evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def _add_leader(leaderboard, score, arr, keep=5):
    if arr is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    leaderboard.append((score, arr))
    leaderboard.sort(key=_tuple_score)
    del leaderboard[max(0, keep):]


def propose_candidate(*args, **kwargs):
    seed = 42
    budget_s = 1800.0
    init_seq = None

    numeric_vals = []
    seq_found = False
    for val in args:
        if isinstance(val, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
            if init_seq is None:
                init_seq = val
                seq_found = True
        elif isinstance(val, (int, float)) and not isinstance(val, bool):
            numeric_vals.append(val)

    if len(numeric_vals) >= 2:
        if isinstance(numeric_vals[0], int):
            seed = int(numeric_vals[0])
        budget_s = float(numeric_vals[-1])
    elif len(numeric_vals) == 1:
        if seq_found:
            budget_s = float(numeric_vals[0])
        else:
            if isinstance(numeric_vals[0], int):
                seed = int(numeric_vals[0])

    if "height_sequence_1" in kwargs and init_seq is None:
        init_seq = kwargs["height_sequence_1"]
    if "init_sequence" in kwargs and init_seq is None:
        init_seq = kwargs["init_sequence"]
    if "init_seq" in kwargs and init_seq is None:
        init_seq = kwargs["init_seq"]
    if "seed" in kwargs:
        sv = kwargs["seed"]
        if isinstance(sv, int) and not isinstance(sv, bool):
            seed = int(sv)
    if "budget_s" in kwargs:
        bv = kwargs["budget_s"]
        if isinstance(bv, (int, float)) and not isinstance(bv, bool):
            budget_s = float(bv)

    try:
        if init_seq is None:
            init_seq = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        init_seq = None

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 0.0:
        budget_s = 1800.0

    init_norm = None
    if init_seq is not None:
        init_norm = _clip_normalize(init_seq)

    if init_norm is not None and init_norm.size >= 2:
        n = init_norm.size
    else:
        n = 4498

    if n < 2:
        n = 64

    F = _next_pow2(max(2 * n - 1, 3 * n - 2))

    # Hard internal deadline leaves time for final direct evaluation.
    hard_deadline = start_time + max(60.0, budget_s)
    run_deadline = hard_deadline - 25.0
    if run_deadline < start_time + 60.0:
        run_deadline = hard_deadline - 5.0

    try:
        gc.disable()
    except Exception:
        pass

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    gen_budget = min(35.0, max(6.0, 0.05 * budget_s))
    gen_deadline = start_time + gen_budget
    if gen_deadline > run_deadline - 60.0:
        gen_deadline = run_deadline - 60.0

    scored = _generate_candidates(n, F, seed, gen_deadline, init_norm)

    if not scored:
        scored.append((2.0, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)))

    starts = []
    if init_norm is not None:
        starts.append(init_norm)

    # Best scoring candidates give the strongest polish targets.
    take = min(6, len(scored))
    for i in range(take):
        starts.append(scored[i][1])

    # Add one randomized candidate to help avoid local minima.
    if len(scored) > take and rng.random() < 1.0:
        idx = int(rng.integers(take, len(scored)))
        starts.append(scored[idx][1])

    # A plain uniform fallback start is harmless and can occasionally seed different dynamics.
    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    starts = starts[:8]

    leaderboard = []
    global_best = None
    global_best_score = np.inf

    remaining = max(0.0, run_deadline - time.monotonic())
    reserve = max(60.0, 0.38 * remaining)
    work_deadline = run_deadline - reserve

    for idx, a0 in enumerate(starts):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= work_deadline:
            break
        rem_starts = len(starts) - idx
        if rem_starts <= 0:
            break
        end = min(work_deadline, now + max(20.0, (work_deadline - now) / float(rem_starts)))
        opt_rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000 * (idx + 1) + 17)
        res_a, res_s = _optimize_shape(a0, F, end, opt_rng)
        print(f"start {idx + 1}/{len(starts)} best_est={res_s:.6f} time_left={run_deadline - time.monotonic():.1f}", flush=True)
        if res_a is not None and np.isfinite(res_s):
            _add_leader(leaderboard, res_s, res_a, keep=6)
            if res_s < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = res_s
                global_best = res_a.copy()

    if global_best is None:
        global_best = scored[0][1].copy()
        global_best_score = scored[0][0]

    # Final refinement from the best sequence found by all starts.
    final_rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 987654321)
    if global_best is not None and time.monotonic() < run_deadline - 30.0:
        res_a, res_s = _optimize_shape(global_best, F, run_deadline, final_rng)
        if res_a is not None and np.isfinite(res_s):
            _add_leader(leaderboard, res_s, res_a, keep=6)
            if res_s < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = res_s
                global_best = res_a.copy()

    print(f"estimated best: {global_best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # Direct verification of a small pool of promising sequences.
    eval_pool = []
    seen_ptrs = set()

    def _pool_add(arr):
        if arr is None:
            return
        ptr = id(arr)
        if ptr in seen_ptrs:
            return
        seen_ptrs.add(ptr)
        eval_pool.append(arr)

    for _, arr in leaderboard:
        _pool_add(arr)
    for i in range(min(5, len(scored))):
        _pool_add(scored[i][1])
    if init_norm is not None:
        _pool_add(init_norm)
    if global_best is not None:
        _pool_add(global_best)
    _pool_add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    eval_limit = hard_deadline - 5.0
    direct_best_score = np.inf
    direct_best_seq = None

    for i, arr in enumerate(eval_pool):
        if time.monotonic() > eval_limit:
            break
        if i > 10:
            break
        sc = _safe_evaluate(arr)
        print(f"direct checked candidate {i + 1}: {sc:.6f}", flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < direct_best_score:
            direct_best_score = sc
            direct_best_seq = arr

    if direct_best_seq is not None:
        final_seq = direct_best_seq
    elif global_best is not None:
        final_seq = global_best
    else:
        final_seq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    final_seq = _clip_normalize(final_seq)
    if final_seq is None:
        final_seq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
